=&({\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{t\top}{\mathbf{A}}^{\top}{\mathbf{A}}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{t})^{-1}\left({\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{t\top}\mathcal{M}_{1}({\mathbfcal{Y}}\times_{1}{\mathbf{A}}^{\top})\otimes_{j=(1+m)}^{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}^{t}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{t\top}{\mathbf{A}}^{\top}{\mathbf{A}}{\mathbf{U}}_{1\perp}^{t}{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{t}\mathcal{M}_{1}({\mathbfcal{S}}^{t})\right)\\
\overset{\eqref{eq: mode-1-part2}}{=}&({\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{t\top}{\mathbf{A}}^{\top}{\mathbf{A}}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{t})^{-1}\\
&\cdot\left({\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{t\top}{\mathbf{A}}^{\top}\mathcal{M}_{1}({\mathbfcal{Y}})\otimes_{j=(1+m)}^{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}^{t}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{t\top}{\mathbf{A}}^{\top}{\mathbf{A}}{\mathbf{U}}_{1\perp}^{t}{\mathbf{U}}_{1\perp}^{t\top}({\mathbf{A}}^{\top}{\mathbf{A}})^{-1}\mathcal{M}_{1}({\mathbfcal{Y}}\times_{1}{\mathbf{A}}^{\top}){\mathbf{W}}_{1}^{t}{\mathbf{V}}_{1}^{t\top}\right)\\
=&({\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{t\top}{\mathbf{A}}^{\top}{\mathbf{A}}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{t})^{-1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{t\top}{\mathbf{A}}^{\top}\\
&\cdot\left(\mathcal{M}_{1}({\mathbfcal{Y}})\otimes_{j=(1+m)}^{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}^{t}-{\mathbf{A}}{\mathbf{U}}_{1\perp}^{t}{\mathbf{U}}_{1\perp}^{t\top}({\mathbf{A}}^{\top}{\mathbf{A}})^{-1}{\mathbf{A}}^{\top}\mathcal{M}_{1}({\mathbfcal{Y}}){\mathbf{W}}^{t}_{1}{\mathbf{V}}^{t\top}_{1}\right).\end{split}

This finishes the proof.
\blacksquare

J
Additional Proofs and Lemmas

Lemma 13

(Tensor Restricted Orthogonal Property)
Let
{\mathbfcal{Z}}_{1},{\mathbfcal{Z}}_{2}\in\mathbb{R}^{p_{1}\times\cdots\times p_{d}}
be two low Tucker rank tensors with
{\rm Tucrank}({\mathbfcal{Z}}_{1})=\mathbf{r}_{1}:=(r_{1},\ldots,r_{d})
,
{\rm Tucrank}({\mathbfcal{Z}}_{2})=\mathbf{r}_{2}:=(r_{1}^{\prime},\ldots,r_{d}^{\prime})
. Then,

|\langle\mathscr{A}({\mathbfcal{Z}}_{1}),\mathscr{A}({\mathbfcal{Z}}_{2})\rangle-\langle{\mathbfcal{Z}}_{1},{\mathbfcal{Z}}_{2}\rangle|\leq R_{\mathbf{r}_{1}+\mathbf{r}_{2}}\|{\mathbfcal{Z}}_{1}\|_{{\rm F}}\|{\mathbfcal{Z}}_{2}\|_{{\rm F}}.

(84)

Proof.
Without loss of generality, assume
\|{\mathbfcal{Z}}_{1}\|_{{\rm F}}=1
,
\|{\mathbfcal{Z}}_{2}\|_{{\rm F}}=1
. Notice that
{\mathbfcal{Z}}_{1}+{\mathbfcal{Z}}_{2}
is of at most Tucker rank
\mathbf{r}_{1}+\mathbf{r}_{2}
as the matricization of
{\mathbfcal{Z}}_{1}+{\mathbfcal{Z}}_{2}
on each mode
k
is of at most rank
r_{k}+r_{k}^{\prime}
. Similarly,
{\mathbfcal{Z}}_{1}-{\mathbfcal{Z}}_{2}
is also at most Tucker rank
\mathbf{r}_{1}+\mathbf{r}_{2}
. Then by the definition of TRIP constant, we have

\begin{split}&(1-R_{\mathbf{r}_{1}+\mathbf{r}_{2}})\|{\mathbfcal{Z}}_{1}\pm{\mathbfcal{Z}}_{2}\|^{2}_{{\rm F}}\leq\|\mathscr{A}({\mathbfcal{Z}}_{1}\pm{\mathbfcal{Z}}_{2})\|_{{\rm F}}^{2}\\
&(1+R_{\mathbf{r}_{1}+\mathbf{r}_{2}})\|{\mathbfcal{Z}}_{1}\pm{\mathbfcal{Z}}_{2}\|^{2}_{{\rm F}}\geq\|\mathscr{A}({\mathbfcal{Z}}_{1}\pm{\mathbfcal{Z}}_{2})\|_{{\rm F}}^{2}.\end{split}

Notice that the above inequalities hold in both settings when
R_{\mathbf{r}_{1}+\mathbf{r}_{2}}>1
and
R_{\mathbf{r}_{1}+\mathbf{r}_{2}}<1
. Then

\begin{split}\|\mathscr{A}({\mathbfcal{Z}}_{1}+{\mathbfcal{Z}}_{2})\|_{\rm F}^{2}-\|\mathscr{A}({\mathbfcal{Z}}_{1}-{\mathbfcal{Z}}_{2})\|_{\rm F}^{2}&=4\langle\mathscr{A}({\mathbfcal{Z}}_{1}),\mathscr{A}({\mathbfcal{Z}}_{2})\rangle\leq 4\langle{\mathbfcal{Z}}_{1},{\mathbfcal{Z}}_{2}\rangle+4R_{\mathbf{r}_{1}+\mathbf{r}_{2}},\\
\|\mathscr{A}({\mathbfcal{Z}}_{1}+{\mathbfcal{Z}}_{2})\|_{\rm F}^{2}-\|\mathscr{A}({\mathbfcal{Z}}_{1}-{\mathbfcal{Z}}_{2})\|_{\rm F}^{2}&=4\langle\mathscr{A}({\mathbfcal{Z}}_{1}),\mathscr{A}({\mathbfcal{Z}}_{2})\rangle\geq 4\langle{\mathbfcal{Z}}_{1},{\mathbfcal{Z}}_{2}\rangle-4R_{\mathbf{r}_{1}+\mathbf{r}_{2}}\end{split}

and this implies (
84
).
\blacksquare

Lemma 14

(Tensor Estimation from Projection
(
Luo and Zhang, 2021
, Lemma 11)
)
Given two order-
d
tensors
{\mathbfcal{Y}},{\mathbfcal{X}}\in\mathbb{R}^{p_{1}\times\cdots\times p_{d}}
. Suppose
{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{0}\in\mathbb{O}_{p_{k},r_{k}}
, then

\left\|{\mathbfcal{Y}}\times_{k=1}^{d}P_{{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{0}}-{\mathbfcal{X}}\right\|_{{\rm F}}\leq\left\|({\mathbfcal{Y}}-{\mathbfcal{X}})\times_{1}P_{{\mathbf{U}}^{0}_{1}}\times\cdots\times_{d}P_{{\mathbf{U}}^{0}_{d}}\right\|_{{\rm F}}+\sum_{k=1}^{d}\left\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k\perp}^{0\top}\mathcal{M}_{k}({\mathbfcal{X}})\right\|_{\rm F}.

Definition 4

Suppose
{\mathscr{T}}
is a subset of a normed space. A set
\mathcal{N}^{{\mathscr{T}}}_{\epsilon}\subseteq{\mathscr{T}}
is called a
\epsilon
-net of
{\mathscr{T}}
with respect to the norm
\|\cdot\|
if for each
{\mathbfcal{T}}\in{\mathscr{T}}
, there exists
{\mathbfcal{T}}_{0}\in\mathcal{N}_{\epsilon}^{\mathscr{T}}
with
\|{\mathbfcal{T}}-{\mathbfcal{T}}_{0}\|\leq\epsilon
. The minimal cardinality of an
\epsilon
-net of
{\mathscr{T}}
with respect to the norm
\|\cdot\|
is denoted by
\mathcal{N}({\mathscr{T}},\|\cdot\|,\epsilon)
and is called the covering number of
{\mathscr{T}}
at scale
\epsilon
.

Next, we give a sharp bound on the covering number for the set of low Tucker rank tensors with respect to the Frobenius norm and it improves upon
(
Rauhut et al., 2017
, Lemma 2)
.

Lemma 15
(Covering Number for Low Tucker Rank Tensors)

Let
0<\epsilon<1
, the covering number of
{\mathscr{T}}_{\mathbf{p},\mathbf{r}}:=\{{\mathbfcal{T}}\in\mathbb{R}^{p_{1}\times\cdots\times p_{d}}:{\rm Tucrank}({\mathbfcal{T}})\leq\mathbf{r}:=(r_{1},\ldots,r_{d}),\|{\mathbfcal{T}}\|_{\rm F}\leq 1\}
with respect to the Frobenious norm satisfies
\mathcal{N}({\mathscr{T}}_{\mathbf{p},\mathbf{r}},\|\cdot\|_{\rm F},\epsilon)\leq\left(\frac{3(d+1)}{\epsilon}\right)^{\prod_{i=1}^{d}r_{i}}\prod_{i=1}^{d}\left(\frac{c_{0}(d+1)}{\epsilon}\right)^{(p_{i}-r_{i})r_{i}}
for some absolute constant
c_{0}>0
.

Proof of Lemma
15
.
Given any
{\mathbfcal{T}}\in{\mathscr{T}}_{\mathbf{p},\mathbf{r}}
,
{\mathbfcal{T}}
has the Tucker decomposition
{\mathbfcal{T}}={\mathbfcal{S}}\times_{i=1}^{d}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}
for some
{\mathbfcal{S}}
satisfying
\|{\mathbfcal{S}}\|_{\rm F}\leq 1
and
{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\in\mathbb{O}_{p_{i},r_{i}}
. We will construct a
\epsilon
-net for
{\mathscr{T}}_{\mathbf{p},\mathbf{r}}
by covering
\{{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{d}
and the set of
r_{1}\times\cdots\times r_{d}
tensors with Frobenius norm at most
1
.

By
(
Zhang and Xia, 2018
, Lemma 7)
, we can construct a
\epsilon/(d+1)
-net (
0<\epsilon/(d+1)<1
)
\{{\mathbfcal{S}}^{(1)},\cdots,{\mathbfcal{S}}^{(N_{{\mathbfcal{S}}})}\}
for
\{{\mathbfcal{S}}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{r_{1}\times\cdots\times r_{d}}:\|{\mathbfcal{S}}^{\prime}\|_{\rm F}\leq 1\}
such that

\sup_{{\mathbfcal{S}}^{\prime}:\|{\mathbfcal{S}}^{\prime}\|_{\rm F}\leq 1}\min_{i\leq N_{{\mathbfcal{S}}}}\|{\mathbfcal{S}}^{\prime}-{\mathbfcal{S}}^{(i)}\|_{\rm F}\leq\epsilon/(d+1)

with
N_{{\mathbfcal{S}}}\leq(\frac{3(d+1)}{\epsilon})^{\prod_{i=1}^{d}r_{i}}
.

At the same time, by
(
Szarek, 1982
, Proposition 8)
, for each
k=1,\ldots,d
, we can construct a
\epsilon/(d+1)
-net
\{{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(1)},\ldots,{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(N_{k})}\}
on the Grassmann manifold of
r_{k}
-dimensional subspaces in
\mathbb{R}^{p_{k}}
with the metric
d({\mathbf{U}}_{1},{\mathbf{U}}_{2})=\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\|_{\rm F}
such that

\sup_{{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\in\mathbb{O}_{p_{k},r_{k}}}\min_{i\leq N_{k}}d({\mathbf{U}}_{k},{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(i)})\leq\epsilon/(d+1)

with
N_{k}\leq(\frac{c_{0}(d+1)}{\epsilon})^{r_{k}(p_{k}-r_{k})}
for some absolute constant
c_{0}>0
.

Given any fixed
{\mathbfcal{T}}\in{\mathscr{T}}_{\mathbf{p},\mathbf{r}}
with Tucker decomposition
{\mathbfcal{S}}\times_{i=1}^{d}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}
, we can find
{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(i_{k})}
in the corresponding
\epsilon/(d+1)
-net such that
d({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(i_{k})},{\mathbf{U}}_{k})\leq\epsilon/(d+1)
. Let
{\mathbf{O}}_{k}=\mathop{\rm arg\min}_{{\mathbf{O}}\in\mathbb{O}_{r_{k}}}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{O}}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(i_{k})}\|_{\rm F}
. By
(
Cai and Zhang, 2018
, Lemma 1)
, we have
\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{O}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(i_{k})}\|_{\rm F}\leq d({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(i_{k})},{\mathbf{U}}_{k})\leq\epsilon/(d+1)
. Denote
\widebar{{\mathbfcal{S}}}={\mathbfcal{S}}\times_{k=1}^{d}{\mathbf{O}}_{k}^{\top}
and let
{\mathbfcal{S}}^{(i_{0})}
be the one in the core tensor
\epsilon/(d+1)
-net such that
\|{\mathbfcal{S}}^{(i_{0})}-\widebar{{\mathbfcal{S}}}\|_{\rm F}\leq\epsilon/(d+1)
. Thus

\begin{split}&\|{\mathbfcal{T}}-{\mathbfcal{S}}^{(i_{0})}\times_{k=1}^{d}{\mathbf{U}}^{(i_{k})}_{k}\|_{\rm F}\\
=&\left\|\widebar{{\mathbfcal{S}}}\times_{k=1}^{d}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{O}}_{k}-{\mathbfcal{S}}^{(i_{0})}\times_{k=1}^{d}{\mathbf{U}}^{(i_{k})}_{k}\right\|_{\rm F}\\
=&\left\|(\widebar{{\mathbfcal{S}}}-{\mathbfcal{S}}^{(i_{0})})\times_{k=1}^{d}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{O}}_{k}+\sum_{k=1}^{d}{\mathbfcal{S}}^{(i_{0})}\times_{j<k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}^{(i_{j})}\times_{k}({\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{O}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}^{(i_{k})}_{k})\times_{j>k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}{\mathbf{O}}_{j}\right\|_{\rm F}\\
\leq&\|(\widebar{{\mathbfcal{S}}}-{\mathbfcal{S}}^{(i_{0})})\times_{k=1}^{d}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{O}}_{k}\|_{\rm F}+\sum_{k=1}^{d}\|{\mathbfcal{S}}^{(i_{0})}\times_{j<k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}^{(i_{j})}\times_{k}({\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{O}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}^{(i_{k})}_{k})\times_{j>k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}{\mathbf{O}}_{j}\|_{\rm F}\\
\leq&(d+1)\epsilon/(d+1)=\epsilon.\end{split}

This finishes the proof of this lemma.
\blacksquare

The following Lemma
16
quantifies the projection error under the perturbation model.

Lemma 16

(A perturbation projection error bound
(
Luo et al., 2021
, Theorem 2)
)
Suppose
{\mathbf{B}}={\mathbf{A}}+{\mathbf{Z}}
for some rank-
r
matrix
{\mathbf{A}}
and perturbation matrix
{\mathbf{Z}}
. Denote the top rank
r
truncated SVD of
{\mathbf{B}}
as
\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}^{\top}
. Then for any
q\in[1,\infty]
,

\max\left\{\|P_{\widehat{{\mathbf{U}}}_{\perp}}{\mathbf{A}}\|_{q},\|{\mathbf{A}}P_{\widehat{{\mathbf{V}}}_{\perp}}\|_{q}\right\}\leq 2\|{\mathbf{Z}}_{\max(r)}\|_{q}.

Here
\|\cdot\|_{q}
denotes the matrix Schatten-
q
norm.
